[6:0] ALU_BREV = 7'b1001001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FF1  = 7'b0110110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_FL1  = 7'b0110111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CNT  = 7'b0110100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLB  = 7'b0110101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXTS = 7'b0111110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EXT  = 7'b0111111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LTS  = 7'b0000000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LTU  = 7'b0000001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LES  = 7'b0000100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_LEU  = 7'b0000101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GTS  = 7'b0001000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GTU  = 7'b0001001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GES  = 7'b0001010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_GEU  = 7'b0001011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_EQ   = 7'b0001100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_NE   = 7'b0001101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLTS = 7'b0000010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLTU = 7'b0000011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLETS= 7'b0000110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SLETU= 7'b0000111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_ABS  = 7'b0010100;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIP = 7'b0010110;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_CLIPU= 7'b0010111;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_INS  = 7'b0101101;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MIN  = 7'b0010000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MINU = 7'b0010001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAX  = 7'b0010010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_MAXU = 7'b0010011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIVU = 7'b0110000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_DIV  = 7'b0110001;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REMU = 7'b0110010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_REM  = 7'b0110011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF = 7'b0111010;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_SHUF2= 7'b0111011;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKLO= 7'b0111000;\n  localparam logic [6:0] ALU_PCKHI= 7'b0111001;\n\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE32 = 2'b00;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE16 = 2'b10;\n  localparam logic [1:0] VEC_MODE8  = 2'b11;\n\n  // mask shared by adder rounding and bit-manipulation\n  logic [31:0] bmask;\n  assign bmask = (~(32'hFFFFFFFE << bmask_a_i)) << bmask_b_i;\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // small helper functions\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [4:0] fl1_func(input [31:0] x);\n    integer j;\n    begin\n      fl1_func = 5'd0;\n      for (j = 0; j < 32; j = j + 1) begin\n        if (x[j]) fl1_func = j[4:0];\n      end\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] ff1_func(input [31:0] x);\n    integer j;\n    begin\n      ff1_func = 5'd0;\n      for (j = 31; j >= 0; j = j - 1) begin\n        if (x[j]) ff1_func = j[4:0];\n      end\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [7:0] byte_mux(input [31:0] word, input [1:0] sel);\n    case (sel)\n      2'd0:    byte_mux = word[7:0];\n      2'd1:    byte_mux = word[15:8];\n      2'd2:    byte_mux = word[23:16];\n      default: byte_mux = word[31:24];\n    endcase\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [15:0] half_mux(input [31:0] word, input sel);\n    half_mux = sel ? word[31:16] : word[15:0];\n  endfunction\n\n  // lane add/sub with no cross-lane carries inside the raw result\n  function automatic [31:0] lane_addsub(input [31:0] a, input [31:0] b,\n                                        input [1:0] mode, input sub);\n    case (mode)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        if (sub)\n          lane_addsub = {a[31:16] - b[31:16], a[15:0] - b[15:0]};\n        else\n          lane_addsub = {a[31:16] + b[31:16], a[15:0] + b[15:0]};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        if (sub)\n          lane_addsub = {a[31:24] - b[31:24],\n                         a[23:16] - b[23:16],\n                         a[15:8]  - b[15:8],\n                         a[7:0]   - b[7:0]};\n        else\n          lane_addsub = {a[31:24] + b[31:24],\n                         a[23:16] + b[23:16],\n                         a[15:8]  + b[15:8],\n                         a[7:0]   + b[7:0]};\n      end\n      default: begin\n        lane_addsub = sub ? (a - b) : (a + b);\n      end\n    endcase\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [31:0] lane_shr(input [31:0] x, input [1:0] mode,\n                                     input [31:0] amt_packed, input arith);\n    logic [31:0] out;\n    begin\n      out = '0;\n      case (mode)\n        VEC_MODE16: begin\n          if (arith) begin\n            out[15:0]  = $signed(x[15:0])  >>> amt_packed[3:0];\n            out[31:16] = $signed(x[31:16]) >>> amt_packed[19:16];\n          end else begin\n            out[15:0]  = x[15:0]  >> amt_packed[3:0];\n            out[31:16] = x[31:16] >> amt_packed[19:16];\n          end\n        end\n        VEC_MODE8: begin\n          if (arith) begin\n            out[7:0]   = $signed(x[7:0])   >>> amt_packed[2:0];\n            out[15:8]  = $signed(x[15:8])  >>> amt_packed[10:8];\n            out[23:16] = $signed(x[23:16]) >>> amt_packed[18:16];\n            out[31:24] = $signed(x[31:24]) >>> amt_packed[26:24];\n          end else begin\n            out[7:0]   = x[7:0]   >> amt_packed[2:0];\n            out[15:8]  = x[15:8]  >> amt_packed[10:8];\n            out[23:16] = x[23:16] >> amt_packed[18:16];\n            out[31:24] = x[31:24] >> amt_packed[26:24];\n          end\n        end\n        default: begin\n          if (arith) out = $signed(x) >>> amt_packed[4:0];\n          else       out = x >> amt_packed[4:0];\n        end\n      endcase\n      lane_shr = out;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [31:0] lane_shl(input [31:0] x, input [1:0] mode,\n                                     input [31:0] amt_packed);\n    case (mode)\n      VEC_MODE16: lane_shl = {x[31:16] << amt_packed[19:16],\n                              x[15:0]  << amt_packed[3:0]};\n      VEC_MODE8:  lane_shl = {x[31:24] << amt_packed[26:24],\n                              x[23:16] << amt_packed[18:16],\n                              x[15:8]  << amt_packed[10:8],\n                              x[7:0]   << amt_packed[2:0]};\n      default:     lane_shl = x << amt_packed[4:0];\n    endcase\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [31:0] ror32_func(input [31:0] x, input [4:0] amt);\n    ror32_func = (amt == 5'd0) ? x : ((x >> amt) | (x << (6'd32 - {1'b0, amt})));\n  endfunction\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // byte-level compare primitives, aggregated like the reference\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [3:0] cmp_signed_bytes;\n  always_comb begin\n    cmp_signed_bytes = 4'b0000;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES,\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLETS, ALU_MIN, ALU_MAX,\n      ALU_ABS, ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: begin\n        case (vector_mode_i)\n          VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed_bytes = 4'b1111;\n          VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed_bytes = 4'b1010;\n          default:    cmp_signed_bytes = 4'b1000;\n        endcase\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] beq;\n  logic [3:0] bgt;\n  genvar gi;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 4; gi = gi + 1) begin : gen_bytecmp\n      assign beq[gi] = (operand_a_i[8*gi +: 8] == operand_b_i[8*gi +: 8]);\n      assign bgt[gi] = $signed({operand_a_i[8*gi+7] & cmp_signed_bytes[gi],\n                                operand_a_i[8*gi +: 8]}) >\n                       $signed({operand_b_i[8*gi+7] & cmp_signed_bytes[gi],\n                                operand_b_i[8*gi +: 8]});\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [3:0] is_equal;\n  logic [3:0] is_greater;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    // 32-bit default\n    is_equal[3:0] = {4{beq[3] & beq[2] & beq[1] & beq[0]}};\n    is_greater[3:0] = {4{bgt[3] | (beq[3] & (bgt[2] |\n                         (beq[2] & (bgt[1] | (beq[1] & bgt[0])))))}};\n\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{beq[0] & beq[1]}};\n        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{beq[2] & beq[3]}};\n        is_greater[1:0] = {2{bgt[1] | (beq[1] & bgt[0])}};\n        is_greater[3:2] = {2{bgt[3] | (beq[3] & bgt[2])}};\n      end\n      VEC_MODE8: begin\n        is_equal   = beq;\n        is_greater = bgt;\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  logic [3:0] cmp_result;\n  always_comb begin\n    cmp_result = is_equal;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EQ:                                  cmp_result = is_equal;\n      ALU_NE:                                  cmp_result = ~is_equal;\n      ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU:                        cmp_result = is_greater;\n      ALU_GES, ALU_GEU:                        cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;\n      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU:    cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);\n      ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU, ALU_LES, ALU_LEU:  cmp_result = ~is_greater;\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // adder / shifter datapath\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic        adder_sub;\n  logic [31:0] adder_a;\n  logic [31:0] adder_b;\n  logic [31:0] adder_raw;\n  logic [31:0] adder_rounded;\n  logic [31:0] adder_shift_amt_packed;\n  logic        adder_shift_arith;\n  logic        adder_shift_use;\n\n  assign adder_sub = (operator_i == ALU_SUB)  || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||\n                     (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR) ||\n                     is_subrot_i;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    adder_a = operand_a_i;\n    adder_b = operand_b_i;\n\n    if (is_subrot_i) begin\n      adder_a = {operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]};\n      adder_b = {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n    end\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    adder_raw = lane_addsub(adder_a, adder_b, vector_mode_i, adder_sub);\n    if (operator_i == ALU_ABS)\n      adder_raw = lane_addsub(~operand_a_i, '0, vector_mode_i, 1'b0);\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if ((operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||\n        (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR))\n      adder_rounded = adder_raw + (bmask >> 1);\n    else\n      adder_rounded = adder_raw;\n  end\n\n  assign adder_shift_use = (operator_i == ALU_ADD)  || (operator_i == ALU_SUB)  ||\n                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||\n                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) ||\n                           (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR)|| (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR);\n\n  assign adder_shift_arith = (operator_i == ALU_ADD) || (operator_i == ALU_SUB) ||\n                             (operator_i == ALU_ADDR)|| (operator_i == ALU_SUBR);\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (is_clpx_i && adder_shift_use)\n      adder_shift_amt_packed = {{14'b0, clpx_shift_i}, {14'b0, clpx_shift_i}};\n    else\n      adder_shift_amt_packed = {4{3'b000, bmask_b_i}};\n  end\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // main result mux\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic [31:0] minmax_b;\n  logic [ 3:0] minmax_sel;\n  logic        do_min;\n  logic [31:0] minmax_result;\n  logic [31:0] clip_result;\n  logic [31:0] pack_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] cnt_result;\n  logic [ 5:0] bitcount_result;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n  logic [31:0] shuf_word;\n  logic [31:0] shuf_res;\n  logic [31:0] div_shifted_a;\n\n  // popcount submodule (small, used by CNT and divider zero check)\n  cv32e40p_popcnt popcnt_i (\n      .in_i    (operand_a_i),\n      .result_o(cnt_result)\n  );\n\n  always_comb begin\n    minmax_b = operand_b_i;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_ABS)\n      minmax_b = lane_addsub(~operand_a_i, '0, vector_mode_i, 1'b0);\n\n    do_min = (operator_i == ALU_MIN)  || (operator_i == ALU_MINU) ||\n             (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) || (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU);\n\n    minmax_sel = is_greater ^ {4{do_min}};\n\n    minmax_result[31:24] = minmax_sel[3] ? operand_a_i[31:24] : minmax_b[31:24];\n    minmax_result[23:16] = minmax_sel[2] ? operand_a_i[23:16] : minmax_b[23:16];\n    minmax_result[15:8]  = minmax_sel[1] ? operand_a_i[15:8]  : minmax_b[15:8];\n    minmax_result[7:0]   = minmax_sel[0] ? operand_a_i[7:0]   : minmax_b[7:0];\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) begin\n      if ($signed(operand_a_i) <= 32'sd0)\n        clip_result = '0;\n      else if ($signed(operand_a_i) > $signed(operand_b_i))\n        clip_result = operand_b_i;\n      else\n        clip_result = operand_a_i;\n    end else if (operator_i == ALU_CLIP) begin\n      if ($signed(operand_a_i) <= $signed(~operand_b_i))\n        clip_result = ~operand_b_i;\n      else if ($signed(operand_a_i) > $signed(operand_b_i))\n        clip_result = operand_b_i;\n      else\n        clip_result = operand_a_i;\n    end else begin\n      clip_result = minmax_result;\n    end\n  end\n\n  // bit-count results\n  always_comb begin\n    bitcount_result = '0;\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_CNT: bitcount_result = cnt_result;\n      ALU_FF1: bitcount_result = (operand_a_i == 32'h0) ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, ff1_func(operand_a_i)};\n      ALU_FL1: bitcount_result = (operand_a_i == 32'h0) ? 6'd32 : {1'b0, fl1_func(operand_a_i)};\n      ALU_CLB: begin\n        if (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n          bitcount_result = 6'd0;\n        else if (operand_a_i[31])\n          bitcount_result = ((~operand_a_i) == 32'h0) ? 6'd31 :\n                            {2'b00, 30 - fl1_func(~operand_a_i)};\n        else\n          bitcount_result = {2'b00, 30 - fl1_func(operand_a_i)};\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // BREV result\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = '0;\n    if (operator_i == ALU_BREV) begin\n      logic [31:0] t;\n      t = operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0];\n\n      case (bmask_a_i[1:0])\n        2'b00, 2'b11: begin\n          integer j;\n          for (j = 0; j < 32; j = j + 1)\n            reverse_result[j] = t[31-j];\n        end\n        2'b01: begin\n          integer j;\n          for (j = 0; j < 16; j = j + 1)\n            reverse_result[2*j +: 2] = {t[31-2*j], t[30-2*j]};\n        end\n        2'b10: begin\n          integer j;\n          for (j = 0; j < 10; j = j + 1)\n            reverse_result[3*j +: 3] = {t[31-3*j], t[30-3*j], t[29-3*j]};\n          reverse_result[31:30] = 2'b00;\n        end\n        default: ;\n      endcase\n    end\n  end\n\n  // shuffle/pack/extract path\n  always_comb begin\n    shuf_word = '0;\n    shuf_res  = '0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS)\n            shuf_res = {{24{operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i[1:0]+7]}},\n                        operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i[1:0] +: 8]};\n          else\n            shuf_res = {24'b0, operand_a_i[8*imm_vec_ext_i[1:0] +: 8]};\n        end else begin\n          if (operator_i == ALU_EXTS)\n            shuf_res = {{16{operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0]+15]}},\n                        operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n          else\n            shuf_res = {16'b0, operand_a_i[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16]};\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_INS: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuf_res = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_res[8*imm_vec_ext_i[1:0] +: 8] = operand_a_i[7:0];\n        end else begin\n          shuf_res = operand_c_i;\n          shuf_res[16*imm_vec_ext_i[0] +: 16] = operand_a_i[15:0];\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuf_res[7:0]   = byte_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[1:0]);\n          shuf_res[15:8]  = byte_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[9:8]);\n          shuf_res[23:16] = byte_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[17:16]);\n          shuf_res[31:24] = byte_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[25:24]);\n        end else begin\n          shuf_res[15:0]  = half_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[0]);\n          shuf_res[31:16] = half_mux(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[16]);\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_SHUF2: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8) begin\n          shuf_res[7:0]   = byte_mux(operand_b_i[2]  ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[1:0]);\n          shuf_res[15:8]  = byte_mux(operand_b_i[10] ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[9:8]);\n          shuf_res[23:16] = byte_mux(operand_b_i[18] ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[17:16]);\n          shuf_res[31:24] = byte_mux(operand_b_i[26] ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[25:24]);\n        end else begin\n          shuf_res[15:0]  = half_mux(operand_b_i[1] ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[0]);\n          shuf_res[31:16] = half_mux(operand_b_i[17] ? operand_a_i : operand_c_i, operand_b_i[16]);\n        end\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKLO: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuf_res = {operand_c_i[31:16], operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0]};\n        else\n          shuf_res = {operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[15:0]};\n      end\n\n      ALU_PCKHI: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE8)\n          shuf_res = {operand_a_i[7:0], operand_b_i[7:0], operand_c_i[15:0]};\n        else\n          shuf_res = {operand_a_i[31:16], operand_b_i[31:16]};\n      end\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  always_comb begin\n    pack_result = shuf_res;\n  end\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // serial divider interface\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic [31:0] div_result;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift;\n  logic        div_valid;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n  assign div_valid  = enable_i & ((operator_i == ALU_DIV)  || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                                  (operator_i == ALU_REM)  || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  // Replicate the reference divider's shift amount computation.\n  always_comb begin\n    if (operand_a_i == 32'h0)\n      div_shift = 6'd32;\n    else if (div_signed & operand_a_i[31]) begin\n      if ((~operand_a_i) == 32'h0)\n        div_shift = 6'd31;\n      else\n        div_shift = {2'b00, 30 - fl1_func(~operand_a_i)};\n    end else begin\n      div_shift = {2'b00, 31 - fl1_func(operand_a_i)};\n    end\n  end\n\n  // The reference ALU feeds the divider with operand_a left-shifted by div_shift.\n  assign div_shifted_a = operand_a_i << div_shift[4:0];\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI      (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI     (rst_n),\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (div_shifted_a),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI(operand_a_i == 32'h0),\n      .OpBSign_SI  (div_signed & operand_a_i[31]),\n      .OpCode_SI   (operator_i[1:0]),\n      .Res_DO      (div_result),\n      .InVld_SI    (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI   (ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO   (div_ready)\n  );\n\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // final result mux\n  // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_SLL: result_o = lane_shl(operand_a_i, vector_mode_i, operand_b_i);\n      ALU_SRL: result_o = lane_shr(operand_a_i, vector_mode_i, operand_b_i, 1'b0);\n      ALU_SRA: result_o = lane_shr(operand_a_i, vector_mode_i, operand_b_i, 1'b1);\n      ALU_ROR: begin\n        if (vector_mode_i == VEC_MODE32)\n          result_o = ror32_func(operand_a_i, operand_b_i[4:0]);\n        else\n          result_o = lane_shr(operand_a_i, vector_mode_i, operand_b_i, 1'b0);\n      end\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR:\n        result_o = lane_shr(adder_rounded, vector_mode_i,\n                            adder_shift_amt_packed, adder_shift_arith);\n\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = operand_a_i & ~bmask;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = operand_a_i | bmask;\n      ALU_BEXTU: result_o = (operand_a_i >> operand_b_i[4:0]) & bmask;\n      ALU_BEXT: begin\n        logic [31:0] t;\n        t = $signed(operand_a_i) >>> operand_b_i[4:0];\n        result_o = (t & bmask) | ({32{t[bmask_a_i]}} & ~bmask);\n      end\n      ALU_BINS: result_o = ((operand_a_i << operand_b_i[4:0]) & bmask) |\n                           (operand_c_i & ~bmask);\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CNT, ALU_CLB:\n        result_o = {26'b0, bitcount_result};\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI, ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS:\n        result_o = pack_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU:\n        result_o = minmax_result;\n\n      ALU_ABS: begin\n        if (is_clpx_i)\n          result_o = {adder_raw[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]};\n        else\n          result_o = minmax_result;\n      end\n\n      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU:\n        result_o = clip_result;\n\n      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU,\n      ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin\n        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};\n        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};\n        result_o[15:8]  = {8{cmp_result[1]}};\n        result_o[7:0]   = {8{cmp_result[0]}};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU:\n        result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};\n\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU:\n        result_o = div_result;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  assign ABComp_S = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule")

  generate
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec
      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]);
      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(
          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}
      ) > $signed(
          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}
      );
    end
  endgenerate

  // generate the real equal and greater than signals that take the vector
  // mode into account
  always_comb begin
    // 32-bit mode
    is_equal[3:0] = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};
    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]
                                            | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]
                                             | (is_equal_vec[1] & (is_greater_vec[0]))))))}};

    case (vector_mode_i)
      VEC_MODE16: begin
        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};
        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};
        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};
        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};
      end

      VEC_MODE8: begin
        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];
        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];
      end

      default: ;  // see default assignment
    endcase
  end

  // generate comparison result
  logic [3:0] cmp_result;

  always_comb begin
    cmp_result = is_equal;
    unique case (operator_i)
      ALU_EQ:                                 cmp_result = is_equal;
      ALU_NE:                                 cmp_result = ~is_equal;
      ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU:                       cmp_result = is_greater;
      ALU_GES, ALU_GEU:                       cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU:   cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);
      ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU, ALU_LES, ALU_LEU: cmp_result = ~is_greater;
      default:                                ;
    endcase
  end

  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];

`timescale 1ns/10ps
// Equivalence testbench for cv32e40p_alu, the CV32E40P (RI5CY) execution-stage ALU.
//
// The design under test runs side by side with the upstream ALU, embedded below as cv32e40p_alu__ref
// (the flattened upstream sources of starting_point.sv with every module renamed). Both receive the same
// stimulus, and at every falling clock edge after reset the design's ready_o must equal the reference's:
// an operation is accepted or completed in exactly the cycles the upstream ALU accepts or completes it.
// When the reference signals ready, result_o must equal the reference's, and comparison_result_o as well
// for the 14 comparison opcodes (for every other opcode that output is a don't-care).
//
// Three stimulus phases, all deterministic:
//   1. vectors.dat: 20,000 directed and seeded-random vectors with expected outputs captured from the
//      pristine upstream RTL (fields: op vm bma bmb ive cpx sbr csh a b c cchk exp_result exp_cmp, hex).
//      The reference is also checked against these expected values, which guards the harness itself.
//   2. a divider sweep: every divisor bit length x every dividend bit length x the four div/rem opcodes,
//      twice, with random low bits and random signs for the signed opcodes (8,192 operations).
//   3. N_RANDOM operations from a fixed-seed xorshift generator, within the driven envelope of the task
//      description (opcode set, vector modes per opcode family, modifier flags per opcode).
//
// Protocol (the EX stage's): rst_n low for the first 5 cycles. For each operation all inputs are driven
// at a falling edge with enable_i=1 and ex_ready_i=1 and held; ready_o is polled at the following falling
// edges (cap 64 -> error); the outputs are checked at the first poll where the reference is ready; then
// enable_i is deasserted for one cycle before the next operation. ready_o is compared at every poll and
// in the idle cycle between operations.
module tb;
  logic clk;
  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  localparam int N_RANDOM = 1_000_000;   // phase 3 length
  localparam int MAX_SHOWN = 10;         // error lines printed; every error is counted

  logic        rst_n, enable_i, ex_ready_i;
  logic [6:0]  op;
  logic [31:0] a, b, c;
  logic [1:0]  vm, ive, csh;
  logic [4:0]  bma, bmb;
  logic        cpx, sbr;
  logic [31:0] result_o, result_ref;
  logic        cmp_o, cmp_ref, ready_o, ready_ref;

  cv32e40p_alu dut (
      .clk(clk), .rst_n(rst_n), .enable_i(enable_i), .operator_i(op),
      .operand_a_i(a), .operand_b_i(b), .operand_c_i(c), .vector_mode_i(vm),
      .bmask_a_i(bma), .bmask_b_i(bmb), .imm_vec_ext_i(ive), .is_clpx_i(cpx), .is_subrot_i(sbr), .clpx_shift_i(csh),
      .result_o(result_o), .comparison_result_o(cmp_o), .ready_o(ready_o), .ex_ready_i(ex_ready_i)
  );
  cv32e40p_alu__ref refm (
      .clk(clk), .rst_n(rst_n), .enable_i(enable_i), .operator_i(op),
      .operand_a_i(a), .operand_b_i(b), .operand_c_i(c), .vector_mode_i(vm),
      .bmask_a_i(bma), .bmask_b_i(bmb), .imm_vec_ext_i(ive), .is_clpx_i(cpx), .is_subrot_i(sbr), .clpx_shift_i(csh),
      .result_o(result_ref), .comparison_result_o(cmp_ref), .ready_o(ready_ref), .ex_ready_i(ex_ready_i)
  );

  // ---- the 57 opcodes (cv32e40p_pkg.sv) and their families ------------------------------------------
  localparam logic [6:0] OPS [0:56] = '{
      7'h18, 7'h19, 7'h1a, 7'h1b, 7'h1c, 7'h1d, 7'h1e, 7'h1f,   // ADD SUB ADDU SUBU ADDR SUBR ADDUR SUBUR
      7'h15, 7'h2e, 7'h2f,                                       // AND OR XOR
      7'h24, 7'h25, 7'h26, 7'h27,                                // SRA SRL ROR SLL
      7'h28, 7'h29, 7'h2a, 7'h2b, 7'h2c, 7'h49,                  // BEXT BEXTU BINS BCLR BSET BREV
      7'h36, 7'h37, 7'h34, 7'h35,                                // FF1 FL1 CNT CLB
      7'h3e, 7'h3f, 7'h2d,                                       // EXTS EXT INS
      7'h00, 7'h01, 7'h04, 7'h05, 7'h08, 7'h09, 7'h0a, 7'h0b, 7'h0c, 7'h0d,   // LTS LTU LES LEU GTS GTU GES GEU EQ NE
      7'h02, 7'h03, 7'h06, 7'h07,                                // SLTS SLTU SLETS SLETU
      7'h10, 7'h11, 7'h12, 7'h13, 7'h14, 7'h16, 7'h17,           // MIN MINU MAX MAXU ABS CLIP CLIPU
      7'h3a, 7'h3b, 7'h38, 7'h39,                                // SHUF SHUF2 PCKLO PCKHI
      7'h30, 7'h31, 7'h32, 7'h33};                               // DIVU DIV REMU REM
  localparam logic [6:0] OP_SUB = 7'h19, OP_ABS = 7'h14, OP_EXTS = 7'h3e, OP_EXT = 7'h3f, OP_INS = 7'h2d;

  function automatic bit is_cmp(input logic [6:0] o);      return o < 7'h0e;                 endfunction
  function automatic bit is_div(input logic [6:0] o);      return o[6:2] == 5'b01100;        endfunction  // DIVU DIV REMU REM
  function automatic bit is_sdiv(input logic [6:0] o);     return o == 7'h31 || o == 7'h33;  endfunction  // DIV REM
  function automatic bit is_addfam(input logic [6:0] o);   return o[6:3] == 4'b0011;         endfunction  // 18..1f
  function automatic bit is_rounding(input logic [6:0] o); return is_addfam(o) && o[2];      endfunction  // ADDR SUBR ADDUR SUBUR
  function automatic bit is_shift(input logic [6:0] o);    return o[6:2] == 5'b01001;        endfunction  // 24..27
  function automatic bit is_bitmanip(input logic [6:0] o); return (o >= 7'h28 && o <= 7'h2c) || o == 7'h49; endfunction
  function automatic bit is_veccmp(input logic [6:0] o);   return is_cmp(o) && !o[1];        endfunction  // not the scalar set-less ops
  function automatic bit is_minmax(input logic [6:0] o);   return o[6:2] == 5'b00100;        endfunction  // 10..13
  function automatic bit is_shuffle(input logic [6:0] o);  return (o >= 7'h38 && o <= 7'h3b) || o == OP_EXTS || o == OP_EXT || o == OP_INS; endfunction
  // vector modes an opcode is driven in: 3 = {32,16,8}, 2 = {16,8}, 1 = {32}
  function automatic int vm_choices(input logic [6:0] o);
    if (is_addfam(o) || is_veccmp(o) || is_minmax(o) || is_shift(o) || o == OP_ABS) return 3;
    if (is_shuffle(o)) return 2;
    return 1;
  endfunction

  // ---- deterministic pseudo-random source (xorshift32, fixed seed) ----------------------------------
  logic [31:0] rs;
  function automatic logic [31:0] xs_next(input logic [31:0] s);
    logic [31:0] x = s;
    x = x ^ (x << 13); x = x ^ (x >> 17); x = x ^ (x << 5);
    return x;
  endfunction
  task automatic draw(output logic [31:0] v);   // advance the generator, return the new state
    rs = xs_next(rs); v = rs;
  endtask
  localparam logic [31:0] CORNERS [0:29] = '{
      32'h00000000, 32'h00000001, 32'h00000002, 32'h00000003, 32'h00000005,
      32'h7FFFFFFF, 32'h80000000, 32'hFFFFFFFF, 32'hFFFFFFFE, 32'h80000001,
      32'h0000FFFF, 32'hFFFF0000, 32'h00010000, 32'h00008000, 32'h00007FFF,
      32'hAAAAAAAA, 32'h55555555, 32'h00FF00FF, 32'hFF00FF00, 32'h01010101,
      32'h7F7F7F7F, 32'h80808080, 32'hFF7F80FE, 32'h0000007F, 32'h00000080,
      32'hDEADBEEF, 32'h12345678, 32'hCAFEF00D, 32'h00000010, 32'hFFFFFF80};
  localparam logic [7:0] LANE_BYTES [0:9] = '{8'h00, 8'h01, 8'h7F, 8'h80, 8'hFF, 8'hFE, 8'h55, 8'hAA, 8'h02, 8'h81};
  // operand distribution of the stimulus generator: raw, corner, byte-lane patterned, small, small negative
  function automatic logic [31:0] rnd32();
    logic [31:0] m, v, k;
    draw(m); m = m % 100;
    draw(v);
    if (m < 35) return v;
    if (m < 55) return CORNERS[v % 30];
    if (m < 75) begin
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin draw(k); v[i*8 +: 8] = LANE_BYTES[k % 10]; end
      return v;
    end
    if (m < 90) return v % 256;
    return -(v % 256);
  endfunction
  function automatic logic [31:0] shift_amt32();
    logic [31:0] m, v;

  logic [31:0] bmask;

  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
  //   ____            _   _ _   _                      _      _       _     _            //
  //  |  _ \ __ _ _ __| |_(_) |_(_) ___  _ __   ___  __| |    / \   __| | __| | ___ _ __  //
  //  | |_) / _` | '__| __| | __| |/ _ \| '_ \ / _ \/ _` |   / _ \ / _` |/ _` |/ _ \ '__| //
  //  |  __/ (_| | |  | |_| | |_| | (_) | | | |  __/ (_| |  / ___ \ (_| | (_| |  __/ |    //
  //  |_|   \__,_|_|   \__|_|\__|_|\___/|_| |_|\___|\__,_| /_/   \_\__,_|\__,_|\___|_|    //
  //                                                                                      //
  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  logic        adder_op_b_negate;
  logic [31:0] adder_op_a, adder_op_b;
  logic [35:0] adder_in_a, adder_in_b;
  logic [31:0] adder_result;
  logic [36:0] adder_result_expanded;


  assign adder_op_b_negate = (operator_i == ALU_SUB) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                             (operator_i == ALU_SUBU) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR) || is_subrot_i;

  // prepare operand a
  assign adder_op_a = (operator_i == ALU_ABS) ? operand_a_neg : (is_subrot_i ? {
    operand_b_i[15:0], operand_a_i[31:16]
  } : operand_a_i);

  // prepare operand b
  assign adder_op_b = adder_op_b_negate ? (is_subrot_i ? ~{
    operand_a_i[15:0], operand_b_i[31:16]
  } : operand_b_neg) : operand_b_i;

  // prepare carry
  always_comb begin
    adder_in_a[0]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[8:1]   = adder_op_a[7:0];
    adder_in_a[9]     = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[17:10] = adder_op_a[15:8];
    adder_in_a[18]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[26:19] = adder_op_a[23:16];
    adder_in_a[27]    = 1'b1;
    adder_in_a[35:28] = adder_op_a[31:24];

    adder_in_b[0]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[8:1]   = adder_op_b[7:0];
    adder_in_b[9]     = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[17:10] = adder_op_b[15:8];
    adder_in_b[18]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[26:19] = adder_op_b[23:16];
    adder_in_b[27]    = 1'b0;
    adder_in_b[35:28] = adder_op_b[31:24];

    if (adder_op_b_negate || (operator_i == ALU_ABS || operator_i == ALU_CLIP)) begin
      // special case for subtractions and absolute number calculations
      adder_in_b[0] = 1'b1;

      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_b[9]  = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[18] = 1'b1;
          adder_in_b[27] = 1'b1;
        end
      endcase

    end else begin
      // take care of partitioning the adder for the addition case
      case (vector_mode_i)
        VEC_MODE16: begin
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
        end

        VEC_MODE8: begin
          adder_in_a[9]  = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[18] = 1'b0;
          adder_in_a[27] = 1'b0;
        end
      endcase
    end
  end

  // actual adder
  assign adder_result_expanded = $signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b);
  assign adder_result = {
    adder_result_expanded[35:28],
    adder_result_expanded[26:19],
    adder_result_expanded[17:10],
    adder_result_expanded[8:1]
  };


  // normalization stage
  logic [31:0] adder_round_value;
  logic [31:0] adder_round_result;

  assign adder_round_value  = ((operator_i == ALU_ADDR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBR) ||
                               (operator_i == ALU_ADDUR) || (operator_i == ALU_SUBUR)) ?
                                {
    1'b0, bmask[31:1]
  } : '0;
  assign adder_round_result = adder_result + adder_round_value;

  // \___ \| |_| || || |_    | |        //

[Step 37/60]
  //  |_| \_\___||___/\__,_|_|\__| |_|  |_|\__,_/_/\_\  //
  //                                                    //
  ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

  always_comb begin
    result_o = '0;

    unique case (operator_i)
      // Standard Operations
      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;
      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;
      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;

      // Shift Operations
      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,
      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR,
      ALU_SLL,
      ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA,
      ALU_ROR:
      result_o = shift_result;

      // bit manipulation instructions
      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;

      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;
      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;

      // Bit reverse instruction
      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;

      // pack and shuffle operations
      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI, ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS: result_o = pack_result;

      // Min/Max/Ins
      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU: result_o = result_minmax;

      //Abs/Cplxconj , ABS is used to do 0 - A for Cplxconj
      ALU_ABS: result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;

      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;

      // Comparison Operations
      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU, ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin
        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};
        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};
        result_o[15:8]  = {8{cmp_result[1]}};
        result_o[7:0]   = {8{cmp_result[0]}};
      end
      // Non-vector comparisons
      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};

      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CLB, ALU_CNT: result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result[5:0]};

      // Division Unit Commands
      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU: result_o = result_div;

      default: ;  // default case to suppress unique warning
    endcase
  end

  assign ready_o = div_ready;

endmodule

module cv32e40p_popcnt (
    input  logic [31:0] in_i,
    output logic [ 5:0] result_o
);

  assign result_o = $countones(in_i);

endmodule

Please write a brief summary covering:
1. What approaches did you try and what worked best?
2. What optimizations had the most impact?

[ASSISTANT]
1. **Approaches tried**
   - Started with the provided `starting_point.sv` as a known-correct baseline.
   - Built a clean direct behavioral rewrite (`alu_direct.sv`) using per-lane functions and a simplified result mux. It passed after fixing the ABS adder path, but synthesized much larger/slower, so it was beaten by the original.
   - Attempted a more aggressive custom rewrite (`alu_custom.sv`), but it failed due to unsupported function constructs/SystemVerilog lint issues and a divider-timing mismatch.
   - The winning path was incremental editing of `starting_point.sv`.

2. **What worked best / most impactful optimizations**
   - Replacing the explicit popcount adder tree with `$countones(in_i)` reduced delay and improved cost.
   - Simplifying the divider `div_shift` path by removing the `clb_result` indirection (`ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : ff1_result - 5'd1`) reduced area/delay slightly while preserving cycle-exact behavior.
   - Simplifying the redundant `operand_b_eq` clip comparator.
   - Separating the `ALU_ROR` case from the combined shift case (while keeping the original shifter datapath) produced the best final result. This last structural change dropped cost to **610734.645912464**.
   - Target delay of around 1000–1370 ps reached the lowest measured area-delay product; 1370 ps gave a near-zero positive slack at the same cost.

3. **What didn’t work or caused regressions**
   - The clean behavioral rewrite (`alu_direct.sv`) had larger area and worse area-delay product despite passing correctness.
   - `alu_custom.sv` failed functionally and had lint/synthesis issues; it was not useful.
   - Directly bypassing the shifter for 32-bit ROR added duplicate rotate logic, increased area/delay, and initially also broke 16-bit ROR behavior.
   - Removing `FL1`/`CLB` from the `shift_left` set increased delay and hurt cost, so it was reverted.
   - Editing the divider corner-case result inside a `case` branch caused syntax errors and was abandoned.

4. **Lessons learned**
   - The best solution was not a rewrite; small, targeted changes to the upstream datapath preserved correctness and let the synthesizer exploit existing structure.
   - Shared datapaths (shifter used by add-right-shift, bit-manip, bit-count, divider) are often better than per-operation bespoke logic.
   - `target_delay` is a secondary knob. Sweeping it only mattered around the critical path; equivalent area-delay was observed at multiple targets.
   - Always evaluate every change, because even minor structural edits can affect delay and area non-monotonically.
   - The final working file is `starting_point.sv` with the incremental edits described above.

================================================================================
